===== LIVE BROCC =====
which='/data/data/com.termux/files/home/broccoli/bin/brocc'
resolved=/data/data/com.termux/files/home/broccoli/bin/brocc
----- brocc source -----
#!/usr/bin/env bash
#
# brocc
#
# Broccoli command router.
#
# Self-contained CLI dispatcher for the currently installed Phase 1
# modules plus compatibility commands referenced by worker handoffs.
#

set -Eeuo pipefail

ROOT="${BROCC_ROOT:-$HOME/broccoli}"

export PATH="$ROOT/bin:$PATH"
export PYTHONPATH="$ROOT${PYTHONPATH:+:$PYTHONPATH}"

cmd="${1:-help}"
shift || true

report()
{
    local report_file="$ROOT/reports/latest.txt"

    if [ ! -f "$report_file" ]; then
        printf 'No report found: %s\n' "$report_file" >&2
        return 1
    fi

    cat "$report_file"
}

show_log()
{
    local count="${1:-40}"

    case "$count" in
        ''|*[!0-9]*)
            printf 'Usage: brocc log [line-count]\n' >&2
            return 2
            ;;
    esac

    local candidates=(
        "$ROOT/logs/broccoli.log"
        "$ROOT/logs/worker.log"
        "$ROOT/broccoli.log"
    )

    local file

    for file in "${candidates[@]}"; do
        if [ -f "$file" ]; then
            tail -n "$count" "$file"
            return 0
        fi
    done

    printf '%s\n' "No canonical log found. Recent files:"
    find "$ROOT" \
        -type f \
        \( -name '*.log' -o -name '*.txt' \) \
        -printf '%T@ %p\n' 2>/dev/null |
        sort -nr |
        head -n 10 |
        cut -d' ' -f2-
}

user_done()
{
    local wait_file="$ROOT/WAITING_USER.txt"

    if [ -e "$wait_file" ]; then
        rm -f -- "$wait_file"
        printf 'WAITING_USER cleared: %s\n' "$wait_file"
    else
        printf 'WAITING_USER already clear.\n'
    fi

    #
    # Compatibility marker. This records the operator acknowledgement
    # without inventing worker state or starting a send cycle.
    #
    mkdir -p "$ROOT/state"

    {
        printf 'timestamp=%s\n' "$(date -Iseconds)"
        printf 'action=user-done\n'
        printf 'pid=%s\n' "$$"
    } > "$ROOT/state/user_done.marker"

    printf 'User completion acknowledged.\n'
}

usage()
{
    cat <<'HELP'
brocc probe
brocc harvest [args...]
brocc read
brocc smoke
brocc cycle
brocc report
brocc log [lines]
brocc user-done
brocc help

Commands:
  probe       inspect current Android/UI state
  harvest     pull chat text into data/harvest/*.jsonl
  read        read current thread
  smoke       Phase 1 harvest smoke operation
  cycle       disabled until harvest is stable
  report      display reports/latest.txt
  log         display recent Broccoli log information
  user-done   clear WAITING_USER compatibility state
HELP
}

case "$cmd" in

    agent)
        sub="${1:-help}"

        case "$sub" in

            scan)
                exec python3 "$ROOT/agent/context.py"
                ;;

            plan)
                exec python3 "$ROOT/agent/planner.py"
                ;;

            verify)
                exec python3 "$ROOT/agent/verifier.py"
                ;;

            loop)
                exec python3 "$ROOT/agent/loop.py"
                ;;

            handoff)
                cat "$ROOT/prompts/next_prompt.md"
                ;;

            *)
                echo "brocc agent scan|plan|verify|loop|handoff"
                ;;
        esac
        ;;


    sysdump)

        sub="${1:-tail}"

        case "$sub" in

            start)

                mkdir -p "$ROOT/logs"

                nohup \
                python3 \
                "$ROOT/daemons/sysdumpd.py" \
                >>"$ROOT/logs/sysdump.log" \
                2>&1 &

                echo "sysdump started"
            ;;


            tail)

                tail -20 \
                "$ROOT/data/sysdump/system.jsonl"
            ;;


            replay)

                cd "$ROOT"

                python3 \
                -m modules.sysdump.replay \
                "${2:-30}"
            ;;

        esac

        ;;

    probe)
        exec python3 "$ROOT/modules/state_probe.py"
        ;;

    harvest)
        exec python3 "$ROOT/modules/chat_harvest.py" "$@"
        ;;

    read)
        export BROCC_NO_SCROLL="${BROCC_NO_SCROLL:-0}"

        exec python3 -c "
import json
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, '$ROOT')

from modules.chat_reader import read_thread

print(json.dumps(read_thread(), indent=2))
"
        ;;

    smoke)
        printf '%s\n' \
            'Phase1: use brocc harvest (no send)'

        exec python3 "$ROOT/modules/chat_harvest.py"
        ;;

    cycle)
        printf '%s\n' \
            'send cycle disabled until harvest stable; use: brocc harvest'

        exit 0
        ;;

    report)
        report
        ;;

    log)
        show_log "${1:-40}"
        ;;

    user-done)
        user_done
        ;;

    help|-h|--help)
        usage
        ;;

    *)
        printf 'unknown: %s\n\n' "$cmd" >&2
        usage >&2
        exit 1
        ;;
esac
